```python
# EVOLVE-BLOCK-START
CONCEPT = ("For C11 semidirect C5 of order 55 and (1,3)x(1,3), independently select three-term components with fewer rooted weight-eight words; test whether five measured distances exceed eight.")
GROUP = ("Keep the measured order-55 nonabelian bed fixed: the parent's five distinct n=550 codes had d30s=8, providing a filling-only comparison.")
SHAPES = ("Keep equal (1,3)x(1,3) sides: n=550, guaranteed k>=220 and rate 0.400, with equal-length three-block components. The parent's lightest X and Z logicals occupied sector-1 Acol/Bcol blocks across the columns.")
FILLING = ("Twenty indexed three-distinct-coset motifs use three terms per cell and row weight 12. Screen independently labelled A and B components on verified lifts, preferring fewer rooted weight-eight words after short-relation screening; check exact k for the final pair.")
FAMILY_NAME = "r040_c11sdC5_13x13_independent_eight_count_v1"

GTYPE = "custom"
CUSTOM_GROUP = """
def valid_q(q):
    return q == 55

def custom_ops(q):
    # x^11=y^5=1, y*x*y^-1=x^3; 3 has order five modulo 11.
    m, r, s = 11, 5, 3
    els = [(a, b) for b in range(r) for a in range(m)]

    def red(g):
        return (g[0] % m, g[1] % r)

    def idx(g):
        a, b = red(g)
        return m * b + a

    def mul(g, h):
        return ((g[0] + pow(s, g[1] % r, m) * h[0]) % m,
                (g[1] + h[1]) % r)

    def inv(g):
        bi = (-g[1]) % r
        return ((-pow(s, bi, m) * g[0]) % m, bi)

    return dict(elements=els, ident=(0, 0), mul=mul, inv=inv,
                idx=idx, red=red)
"""
A0 = None                      # only used by GTYPE = "abelian"

A_SHAPE = (1, 3)               # (ra, ca)
B_SHAPE = (1, 3)               # (rb, cb)


def _component_columns(rows, starts, q):
    """Transpose selected q-row blocks of a verified lifted matrix."""
    columns = [0] * (len(starts) * q)
    mask = (1 << q) - 1
    for check, row in enumerate(rows[:q]):
        for block, start in enumerate(starts):
            bits = (row >> start) & mask
            while bits:
                low = bits & -bits
                columns[block * q + low.bit_length() - 1] |= 1 << check
                bits ^= low
    return columns


def _short_relations(columns):
    """Cheap screen before searching for longer component words."""
    duplicates = len(columns) - len(set(columns))
    pairs = set()
    fours = overlaps = 0
    for i, left in enumerate(columns):
        for right in columns[i + 1:]:
            syndrome = left ^ right
            fours += syndrome in pairs
            pairs.add(syndrome)
            common = (left & right).bit_count()
            overlaps += common * (common - 1) // 2
    return duplicates, fours, overlaps


def _rooted_word(columns, q, limit, node_cap):
    """Search component kernels with one qubit rooted at group identity.

    The left and right regular component lifts each admit a regular
    translation of all check and variable labels. A word touching a
    given variable block therefore has a translate rooted at its
    identity variable. Covering an odd check at every step is complete
    for a zero-syndrome word within the search's node budget.
    """
    adjacency = [[] for _ in range(q)]
    for v, syndrome in enumerate(columns):
        bits = syndrome
        while bits:
            low = bits & -bits
            adjacency[low.bit_length() - 1].append(v)
            bits ^= low

    best = limit + 2
    for block in range(3):
        root = block * q  # identity is element index zero
        budget = [node_cap]

        def visit(syndrome, used, depth):
            nonlocal best
            if not syndrome:
                best = min(best, depth)
                return
            if depth >= limit or syndrome.bit_count() > 3 * (limit - depth):
                return
            if budget[0] <= 0:
                return
            budget[0] -= 1

            # Every completion must include a variable incident to
            # each currently odd check. Choose the tightest of a few.
            bits = syndrome
            pivot = None
            smallest = len(columns) + 1
            for _ in range(min(4, bits.bit_count())):
                low = bits & -bits
                check = low.bit_length() - 1
                bits ^= low
                available = sum(not (used >> v) & 1
                                for v in adjacency[check])
                if available < smallest:
                    smallest, pivot = available, check
            if not smallest:
                return

            options = [v for v in adjacency[pivot]
                       if not (used >> v) & 1]
            options.sort(key=lambda v: (columns[v] & syndrome).bit_count(),
                         reverse=True)
            for v in options:
                next_syndrome = syndrome ^ columns[v]
                if next_syndrome.bit_count() <= 3 * (limit - depth - 1):
                    visit(next_syndrome, used | (1 << v), depth + 1)
                    if best <= 4:
                        return

        visit(columns[root], 1 << root, 1)
        if best <= 4:
            break
    return best


def _rooted_eight_grade(columns, q, node_cap, hit_cap=120):
    """Bounded component search, retaining distinct rooted short words."""
    adjacency = [[] for _ in range(q)]
    for v, syndrome in enumerate(columns):
        bits = syndrome
        while bits:
            low = bits & -bits
            adjacency[low.bit_length() - 1].append(v)
            bits ^= low

    minimum = [10]
    found_eights = set()
    for block in range(3):
        root = block * q                 # identity has index zero
        remaining = [node_cap]

        def visit(syndrome, used, depth):
            if not syndrome:
                minimum[0] = min(minimum[0], depth)
                if depth == 8:
                    found_eights.add(used)
                return
            if depth >= 8 or remaining[0] <= 0:
                return
            if syndrome.bit_count() > 3 * (8 - depth):
                return
            if minimum[0] <= 6 or len(found_eights) >= hit_cap:
                return
            remaining[0] -= 1

            # A completion must cover every currently odd check.
            bits = syndrome
            pivot = None
            fewest = len(columns) + 1
            for _ in range(min(4, bits.bit_count())):
                low = bits & -bits
                check = low.bit_length() - 1
                bits ^= low
                available = sum(not ((used >> v) & 1)
                                for v in adjacency[check])
                if available < fewest:
                    fewest, pivot = available, check
            if not fewest:
                return

            options = [v for v in adjacency[pivot]
                       if not ((used >> v) & 1)]
            options.sort(key=lambda v: (columns[v] & syndrome).bit_count(),
                         reverse=True)
            for v in options:
                next_syndrome = syndrome ^ columns[v]
                if next_syndrome.bit_count() <= 3 * (7 - depth):
                    visit(next_syndrome, used | (1 << v), depth + 1)
                if minimum[0] <= 6 or len(found_eights) >= hit_cap:
                    return

        visit(columns[root], 1 << root, 1)
        if minimum[0] <= 6 or len(found_eights) >= hit_cap:
            break
    return minimum[0], len(found_eights)


def _motif_cell(rng, cosets):
    """Sample without replacement within each prescribed action coset."""
    cell = []
    for b in sorted(set(cosets)):
        cell.extend((a, b) for a in
                    rng.sample(range(11), cosets.count(b)))
    rng.shuffle(cell)
    return cell


def NEW_CTX(rng, G, idx):
    """Select independently labelled A and B components of one motif."""
    from itertools import combinations

    triples = tuple(combinations(range(5), 3))
    design = idx % 20
    family, offset = divmod(design, 5)
    proposals = {"A": [], "B": []}
    q = G.q

    for _ in range(28):
        grids = {}
        for side in ("A", "B"):
            row = []
            for j in range(3):
                pattern = (2 * offset + 3 * family +
                           j * (family + 1) +
                           (4 if side == "B" else 0)) % 10
                shift = rng.randrange(5)
                cosets = tuple((b + shift) % 5
                               for b in triples[pattern])
                row.append(_motif_cell(rng, cosets))
            grids[side] = [row]

        hx, hz, _ = lifted_matrices(G, grids["A"], grids["B"])
        acol = _component_columns(hx, [0, 3*q, 6*q], q)
        bcol = _component_columns(hz, [0, q, 2*q], q)
        for side, columns in (("A", acol), ("B", bcol)):
            coarse = _short_relations(columns)
            proposals[side].append(
                (coarse + (rng.random(),), grids[side], columns))

    selected = {}
    for side in ("A", "B"):
        proposals[side].sort(key=lambda item: item[0])
        finalists = []
        for coarse, grid, columns in proposals[side][:8]:
            minimum, hits = _rooted_eight_grade(columns, q, 1800)
            finalists.append(((minimum, -hits, -coarse[2]),
                              grid, columns))
        finalists.sort(key=lambda item: item[0], reverse=True)

        selected[side] = []
        for _, grid, columns in finalists[:4]:
            minimum, hits = _rooted_eight_grade(columns, q, 12000)
            selected[side].append(
                ((minimum, -hits, rng.random()), grid))
        selected[side].sort(key=lambda item: item[0], reverse=True)

    best = None
    for a_grade, A in selected["A"][:3]:
        for b_grade, B in selected["B"][:3]:
            k = lifted_k(G, A, B)
            key = (220 <= k <= 233,
                   min(a_grade[0], b_grade[0]),
                   a_grade[0] + b_grade[0],
                   max(a_grade[1], b_grade[1]),
                   a_grade[1] + b_grade[1],
                   k, rng.random())
            if best is None or key > best[0]:
                best = (key, {"A": A, "B": B})
    return best[1]


def CELL(rng, G, side, i, j, ctx):
    return list(ctx[side][i][j])
```

```python
# EVOLVE-BLOCK-START
CONCEPT = ("On metacyclic Z13 semidirect_3 Z9 of order 117 with (1,2)x(1,2) sides, independently screened four-term sector-one components can improve the five-code rate-0.200 distance score.")
GROUP = ("Keep m=13, r=9, s=3 and order 117: the measured orientations both give n=585, and the current order-117 bed leads this rate cell. Its order-39 quotient remains available for component screening.")
SHAPES = ("Use equal (1,2)x(1,2) sides: n=5*117=585 and k>=117 guarantee rate 0.200 and equal-length two-column components. The parent's lightest logicals were exclusively in sector-two Arow/Brow blocks; this orientation tests the corresponding sector-one Acol/Bcol route.")
FILLING = ("Use four distinct terms in each cell, making both HX and HZ row sums 12. Twenty indexed action-class motifs receive fresh central-stratified labels and cyclic-gap constraints. Screen verified order-39 sector-one components through weight seven, then check full-lift rank and neighbour overlap; target the parent's four weight-23 readings without seeking extra k.")
FAMILY_NAME = "r020_m13r9s3_12x12_fresh_sector1_gap_screen"

GTYPE = "custom"
CUSTOM_GROUP = """
def valid_q(q):
    return q == 117

def custom_ops(q):
    # Metacyclic Z_13 x| Z_9: y*x*y^-1=x^3; 3^9=1 mod 13.
    m, r, s = 13, 9, 3
    els = [(a, b) for b in range(r) for a in range(m)]

    def mul(g, h):
        return ((g[0] + pow(s, g[1], m) * h[0]) % m, (g[1] + h[1]) % r)

    def inv(g):
        bi = (-g[1]) % r
        return ((-pow(s, bi, m) * g[0]) % m, bi)

    return dict(elements=els, ident=(0, 0), mul=mul, inv=inv)
"""
A0 = None                      # only used by GTYPE = "abelian"

A_SHAPE = (1, 2)               # (ra, ca)
B_SHAPE = (1, 2)               # (rb, cb)


def _quotient_group():
    """The order-39 quotient by the central subgroup generated by y^3."""
    els = [(a, b) for b in range(3) for a in range(13)]

    def mul(g, h):
        return ((g[0] + pow(3, g[1], 13) * h[0]) % 13,
                (g[1] + h[1]) % 3)

    def inv(g):
        bi = (-g[1]) % 3
        return ((-pow(3, bi, 13) * g[0]) % 13, bi)

    return _G(dict(elements=els, ident=(0, 0), mul=mul, inv=inv), 39)


def _four_term_cell(rng, extra_class, spread_mode):
    """Give each action class a term and one class an independent fourth."""
    heights = rng.sample(range(3), 3)
    b_values = [c + 3 * heights[c] for c in range(3)]
    alternatives = [extra_class + 3 * h for h in range(3)
                    if h != heights[extra_class]]
    b_values.append(rng.choice(alternatives))

    # Alternate strict and relaxed C13 difference spreads across slots.
    required_gaps = (6, 5, 6)[spread_mode]
    while True:
        a_values = rng.sample(range(13), 4)
        gaps = {
            min((a_values[i] - a_values[j]) % 13,
                (a_values[j] - a_values[i]) % 13)
            for i in range(4) for j in range(i)
        }
        if len(gaps) >= required_gaps:
            cell = list(zip(a_values, b_values))
            rng.shuffle(cell)
            return cell


def _component_columns(hx, hz, q):
    """Extract both sector-one components from the verified lift."""
    mask = (1 << q) - 1
    result = []
    # For (1,2)x(1,2), HX's first q rows expose A at sector-one
    # bases 0 and 2q; HZ's first q rows expose B at 0 and q.
    for rows, bases in ((hx, (0, 2 * q)), (hz, (0, q))):
        columns = [0] * (2 * q)
        for r in range(q):
            for j, base in enumerate(bases):
                bits = (rows[r] >> base) & mask
                while bits:
                    bit = bits & -bits
                    columns[j * q + bit.bit_length() - 1] |= 1 << r
                    bits ^= bit
        result.append(columns)
    return result


def _shared_neighbour_penalty(columns):
    penalty = 0
    for i, x in enumerate(columns):
        for y in columns[:i]:
            shared = (x & y).bit_count()
            if shared >= 2:
                penalty += shared * (shared - 1) // 2
    return penalty


def _component_signatures(hx, hz, q):
    """Extract sector-two A^T and B^T checks from the verified CSS lifts."""
    rb, cb = B_SHAPE
    n1 = A_SHAPE[1] * cb * q
    a = [0] * (A_SHAPE[0] * q)
    b = [0] * (rb * q)
    for j in range(A_SHAPE[1]):
        for r in range(q):
            bits = hz[(j * rb) * q + r] >> n1
            check = 1 << (j * q + r)
            while bits:
                bit = bits & -bits
                t = bit.bit_length() - 1
                block, position = divmod(t, q)
                if block % rb == 0:
                    a[(block // rb) * q + position] |= check
                bits ^= bit
    for beta in range(cb):
        for r in range(q):
            bits = hx[beta * q + r] >> n1
            check = 1 << (beta * q + r)
            while bits:
                bit = bits & -bits
                t = bit.bit_length() - 1
                block, position = divmod(t, q)
                if block < rb:
                    b[block * q + position] |= check
                bits ^= bit
    return a, b


def _short_component_kernel(columns):
    """Return the first possible kernel weight through six, or seven."""
    length = len(columns)
    singles = set(columns)
    if 0 in singles:
        return 1
    if len(singles) != length:
        return 2
    pairs = {}
    for i in range(length):
        ci = columns[i]
        for j in range(i + 1, length):
            syndrome = ci ^ columns[j]
            if syndrome in singles:
                return 3
            if syndrome in pairs:
                return 4
            pairs[syndrome] = (i, j)
    triples = set()
    found_six = False
    for i in range(length):
        ci = columns[i]
        for j in range(i + 1, length):
            cij = ci ^ columns[j]
            for k in range(j + 1, length):
                syndrome = cij ^ columns[k]
                if syndrome in pairs:
                    return 5
                if syndrome in triples:
                    found_six = True
                else:
                    triples.add(syndrome)
    return 6 if found_six else 7


def _has_quotient_seven(columns, q):
    """Test weight seven, using translation to fix one of two block pivots.

    This is called only after weights through six have been excluded.
    Consequently distinct triples cannot share a syndrome, so one
    representative per syndrome suffices for the disjointness test.
    """
    triples = {}
    length = len(columns)
    for i in range(length - 2):
        first = columns[i]
        ibit = 1 << i
        for j in range(i + 1, length - 1):
            partial = first ^ columns[j]
            ijbits = ibit | (1 << j)
            for k in range(j + 1, length):
                triples[partial ^ columns[k]] = ijbits | (1 << k)

    for pivot in (0, q):
        pbit = 1 << pivot
        for i in range(length - 2):
            if i == pivot:
                continue
            first = columns[pivot] ^ columns[i]
            ibits = pbit | (1 << i)
            for j in range(i + 1, length - 1):
                if j == pivot:
                    continue
                partial = first ^ columns[j]
                ijbits = ibits | (1 << j)
                for k in range(j + 1, length):
                    if k == pivot:
                        continue
                    used = ijbits | (1 << k)
                    other = triples.get(partial ^ columns[k])
                    if other is not None and not (other & used):
                        return True
    return False


def NEW_CTX(rng, G, idx):
    """Screen independent motifs through quotient weight seven and full rank."""
    quotient = _quotient_group()
    signature = tuple((idx // (3 ** position)) % 3
                      for position in range(4))
    spread_mode = (idx // 9) % 3
    shortlist = []

    for _ in range(26):
        cells = {
            "A": [[_four_term_cell(rng, signature[j], spread_mode)
                   for j in range(2)]],
            "B": [[_four_term_cell(rng, signature[2 + j], spread_mode)
                   for j in range(2)]],
        }
        projected = {
            side: [[[(a, b % 3) for a, b in cell] for cell in row]
                   for row in cells[side]]
            for side in ("A", "B")
        }
        hx, hz, _ = lifted_matrices(
            quotient, projected["A"], projected["B"])
        acols, bcols = _component_columns(hx, hz, quotient.q)
        da = _short_component_kernel(acols)
        db = _short_component_kernel(bcols)
        penalty = (_shared_neighbour_penalty(acols)
                   + _shared_neighbour_penalty(bcols))
        preliminary = (min(da, db), da + db, -penalty, rng.random())
        shortlist.append((preliminary, cells, acols, bcols, da, db))

    screened = []
    for _, cells, acols, bcols, da, db in sorted(
            shortlist, key=lambda entry: entry[0], reverse=True)[:12]:
        qa = (8 if da == 7
              and not _has_quotient_seven(acols, quotient.q) else da)
        qb = (8 if db == 7
              and not _has_quotient_seven(bcols, quotient.q) else db)
        penalty = (_shared_neighbour_penalty(acols)
                   + _shared_neighbour_penalty(bcols))
        quotient_grade = (min(qa, qb), qa + qb, -penalty, rng.random())
        screened.append((quotient_grade, cells))

    best, best_grade = None, None
    for quotient_grade, cells in sorted(
            screened, key=lambda entry: entry[0], reverse=True)[:6]:
        hx, hz, n = lifted_matrices(G, cells["A"], cells["B"])
        k = n - gf2_rank(hx) - gf2_rank(hz)
        acols, bcols = _component_columns(hx, hz, G.q)
        full_penalty = (_shared_neighbour_penalty(acols)
                        + _shared_neighbour_penalty(bcols))
        grade = (k <= 131, quotient_grade[0], quotient_grade[1],
                 -full_penalty, quotient_grade[2], -abs(k - 121))
        if best_grade is None or grade > best_grade:
            best, best_grade = cells, grade
    return best


def CELL(rng, G, side, i, j, ctx):
    return list(ctx[side][i][j])
```

Author reasoning for this program: **Evaluating component structure** I'm considering a better shape for a component, possibly a (1,2)x(3,4) configuration. I wonder if I can achieve a relatively significant lift on a small query of 46, and whether it's practical to set a constant rate at .09. My thoughts are going towards how a 1x2 code could be structured with two columns, checking if a small kernel could effectively handle a binomial approach per cell. It might lead to a cycle of 46. **Evaluating system design and ranking** I'm looking at our existing schedule for designs and thinking about how to swap labels A and B. There might be code collapse if they share the same labels — that's good to keep in mind. I need to sharpen the `_kernel_probe` for word elimination; I wonder if a minimum output of 3 for all is feasible. I’ll also explore rows and columns for ranking, as well as conditional checks for k values to avoid errors. I'll aim for a structured approach to organize the profiles efficiently. **Evaluating potential coding issues** I'm considering a potential issue: the transposed code might actually just be an iso of the old code, with a sector swap and an X/Z swap. This could mean the score remains the same at 59.46 if there are duplicated counts but labeled independently. I’m also wondering about the chance of d29 and if max d28 could serve as an upper bound due to column support. If that's the case, there might not be any improvement.

```python
# EVOLVE-BLOCK-START
CONCEPT = ("On cyclic C23 x C2 of order 46 with A=B=(3,2), screening independently labelled three-check component dependencies should produce five d30s readings of at least 29.")
GROUP = ("Keep direct-product C23 x C2 at order 46: the parent scored 59.5, and the transposed shapes retain n=598 for a direct shape comparison.")
SHAPES = ("Equal (3,2)x(3,2) sides have equal-length three-check, two-column components. Their bound k>=46 gives rate 46/598 in cell 0.100. The parent's lightest logicals occupied sector-1 Acol/Bcol blocks; the new orientation tests sector-2 Arow/Brow dependencies.")
FILLING = ("Transpose indexed seven-term-row support profiles, including four-term/singleton cells, and independently sample their labels. New row totals are at most five and opposing column totals are seven, keeping both check weights <=12. Screen verified component check dependencies and limit k above 46 to target the parent's weight-27/28 witnesses.")
FAMILY_NAME = "r010_c23xc2_32x32_transposed_dependency_screen"

GTYPE = "custom"
CUSTOM_GROUP = """
def valid_q(q):
    return q == 46

def custom_ops(q):
    # Direct product C23 x C2, isomorphic to cyclic C46; NOT dihedral.
    m, r = 23, 2
    els = [(a, b) for b in range(r) for a in range(m)]

    def red(g):
        return (g[0] % m, g[1] % r)

    def idx(g):
        a, b = red(g)
        return m * b + a

    def mul(g, h):
        return ((g[0] + h[0]) % m, (g[1] + h[1]) % r)

    def inv(g):
        return ((-g[0]) % m, (-g[1]) % r)

    return dict(elements=els, ident=(0, 0), mul=mul, inv=inv,
                idx=idx, red=red)
"""
A0 = None                      # only used by GTYPE = "abelian"

A_SHAPE = (3, 2)               # (ra, ca)
B_SHAPE = (3, 2)               # (rb, cb)


def _component_columns(G, A, B):
    """Extract both three-check component row families from the verified lift."""
    hx, hz, _ = lifted_matrices(G, A, B)
    q = G.q
    mask = (1 << q) - 1
    a_rows, b_rows = [], []
    for i in range(3):
        for r in range(q):
            # Fix B-column zero and retain the two A-column blocks.
            row = hx[(2 * i) * q + r]
            a_rows.append((row & mask) |
                          (((row >> (2 * q)) & mask) << q))
    for alpha in range(3):
        for r in range(q):
            # Fix A-column zero and retain the two B-column blocks.
            b_rows.append(hz[alpha * q + r] & ((1 << (2 * q)) - 1))
    return a_rows, b_rows


def _component_obstructions(columns):
    """Count short zero-syndrome relations and shared check-neighbour pairs."""
    singleton = set(columns)
    pairs = {}
    triples = fours = overlaps = 0
    for i, x in enumerate(columns):
        for j in range(i):
            y = columns[j]
            syndrome = x ^ y
            if syndrome in singleton:
                triples += 1
            previous = pairs.get(syndrome)
            if previous is not None:
                # Shared-column collisions correspond to shorter relations.
                if previous[0] != i and previous[0] != j \
                        and previous[1] != i and previous[1] != j:
                    fours += 1
            else:
                pairs[syndrome] = (i, j)
            shared = (x & y).bit_count()
            if shared >= 2:
                overlaps += shared * (shared - 1) // 2
    return triples, fours, overlaps


def _cyclic_cell(rng, count, mode):
    """Draw distinct labels with count-dependent C2-coset occupancy."""
    coset = rng.randrange(2)
    if count == 1:
        return [(rng.randrange(23), coset)]
    if count == 2:
        a = rng.randrange(23)
        gap = rng.randrange(1, 23)
        other = coset if mode == 3 and rng.randrange(3) == 0 else 1 - coset
        return [(a, coset), ((a + gap) % 23, other)]
    if count == 3:
        a, b = rng.sample(range(23), 2)
        return [(a, coset), (b, coset),
                (rng.randrange(23), 1 - coset)]
    # Both 2+2 and 3+1 patterns are distinct from a pair/triple cell.
    same = 2 if mode % 2 == 0 else 3
    return ([(a, coset) for a in rng.sample(range(23), same)]
            + [(a, 1 - coset)
               for a in rng.sample(range(23), count - same)])


def _kernel_probe(columns, rng):
    """Probe verified kernel words and pair them with all 46 translations."""
    found = set()
    for _ in range(6):
        order = list(range(len(columns)))
        rng.shuffle(order)
        pivots = {}
        for j in order:
            syndrome, word = columns[j], 1 << j
            while syndrome:
                p = syndrome.bit_length() - 1
                entry = pivots.get(p)
                if entry is None:
                    pivots[p] = (syndrome, word)
                    break
                syndrome ^= entry[0]
                word ^= entry[1]
            else:
                found.add(word)

    short = sorted(found, key=int.bit_count)[:14]
    if not short:
        return (len(columns) + 1, 0, 0)
    minimum = min(word.bit_count() for word in short)
    hits8 = hits9 = 0
    mask = (1 << 23) - 1

    def translate(word, shift, flip):
        result = 0
        for block in range(3):
            for coset in range(2):
                part = (word >> (46 * block + 23 * coset)) & mask
                part = ((part << shift) | (part >> (23 - shift))) & mask
                result |= part << (46 * block + 23 * (coset ^ flip))
        return result

    for i, left in enumerate(short):
        for right in short[:i]:
            for shift in range(23):
                for flip in range(2):
                    combined = left ^ translate(right, shift, flip)
                    if not combined:
                        continue
                    weight = combined.bit_count()
                    minimum = min(minimum, weight)
                    hits8 += weight <= 8
                    hits9 += weight <= 9
    return (minimum, -hits8, -hits9)


def NEW_CTX(rng, G, idx):
    """Screen fresh transposed-profile designs for both component dependencies."""
    original_profiles = (
        ((3, 2, 2), (2, 3, 2)),
        ((4, 2, 1), (1, 2, 4)),
        ((4, 2, 1), (1, 3, 3)),
        ((3, 3, 1), (2, 2, 3)),
    )
    # Old rows total seven and old columns total at most five.
    # After transposition these are respectively opposing-column and
    # component-row totals, bounding HX and HZ weights by 7+5.
    schedule = ((2, 2), (2, 1), (1, 2), (0, 2),
                (2, 0), (2, 3), (3, 2), (1, 1),
                (0, 0), (3, 3), (1, 3), (3, 1),
                (0, 1), (1, 0), (0, 3), (3, 0),
                (2, 2), (1, 2), (2, 1), (3, 2))
    a_profile, b_profile = schedule[idx % len(schedule)]
    supports = {
        "A": [list(column) for column in zip(*original_profiles[a_profile])],
        "B": [list(column) for column in zip(*original_profiles[b_profile])],
    }
    assert _grid_weights(supports["A"], supports["B"]) <= W_MAX
    mode = (idx // 5 + idx % 4) % 4
    shortlist = []

    for _ in range(36):
        cells = {
            side: [[_cyclic_cell(rng, count, mode + (side == "B"))
                    for count in row] for row in supports[side]]
            for side in ("A", "B")
        }
        ac, bc = _component_columns(G, cells["A"], cells["B"])
        da, db = _kernel_probe(ac, rng), _kernel_probe(bc, rng)
        a3, a4, ao = _component_obstructions(ac)
        b3, b4, bo = _component_obstructions(bc)
        grade = (min(da[0], db[0]), da[0] + db[0],
                 da[1] + db[1], da[2] + db[2],
                 -(a3 + b3), -(a4 + b4), -(ao + bo))
        shortlist.append((grade, cells))

    best = None
    for grade, cells in sorted(shortlist, key=lambda x: x[0],
                               reverse=True)[:8]:
        k = lifted_k(G, cells["A"], cells["B"])
        if k > 55:
            continue
        quality = (grade[0], grade[1], grade[2], grade[3],
                   -max(0, k - 48), grade[4], grade[5],
                   grade[6], k)
        if best is None or quality > best[0]:
            best = (quality, cells)
    if best is None:
        raise ValueError("no screened transposed filling kept k near its bound")
    return best[1]


def CELL(rng, G, side, i, j, ctx):
    """Return a copy of the selected complete cell."""
    return list(ctx[side][i][j])

# REFUTED LEDGER (append-only; one line per hypothesis killed by
# measured evidence - never delete or rewrite existing lines):
# (empty)
# REFUTED: Uniform rolled dihedral-58 (1,3)x(1,3) fillings do not reliably exceed distance 4: all five measured parent slots had d30s=4.
# REFUTED: Maximizing noncommutator weight among eight action-spanning labelings does not reliably suppress short witnesses: the order-117 parent had numerous d2s=9 slots and a measured d30s=18 slot.
# REFUTED: Penalizing component relations alone eliminates all short logicals on the order-117 bed: the current program still has many distinct d2s=9 slots.
# REFUTED: The dihedral (2,3)x(2,3) component-obstruction screen raises measured distance beyond 26: all five measured slots remained at d30s=26.
# REFUTED: Cross-coset two-/three-term fillings on cyclic order 46 raise measured distance beyond 27: the current program's five measured slots all had d30s=27.
# REFUTED: Mixed four-term/singleton profiles on cyclic order 46 consistently exceed distance 28: the parent measured four d30s=28 slots and one d30s=27 slot.
# EVOLVE-BLOCK-END
```

# Current Program
```python
"""Initial program: lifted-product CSS code, ONE (group, shape) bed per
program, N_SLOTS (env CM_NSLOTS) freely-filled candidates per generation.

SEARCH SPACE (the only constraints; everything else is free):
- The group and the protograph shapes are fixed for the whole program
  (genotype, editable between generations). Each of the 20 slots fills
  the cells however it wants: PER SLOT, every cell holds 0..5 DISTINCT
  group elements (an empty cell is a zero block) - term counts AND
  elements are both the slot's choice. Identical fillings are
  deduplicated downstream and waste the slot.
- Size law: n = (ca*cb + ra*rb) * q for A of shape (ra, ca), B of shape
  (rb, cb), |G| = q. n must land in [CM_NMIN, CM_NMAX) (default
  [100, 600)); the scaffold picks the largest valid q.
- PER-SLOT validity (scaffold-checked; a violating slot is voided, not
  repaired): max row weight <= CM_WMAX (an HX row sums A-row i with
  B-column j; an HZ row sums a B-row with an A-column - check BOTH),
  no all-zero protograph row or column on either side (a zero A-row
  leaves its sector-2 block untouched by every Z check, which is a
  weight-1 logical - d=1 - unless the matching B column is a single
  1-term cell; symmetric for the other three cases), cells distinct,
  sizes 0..5.
- k > 0 required; k is measured on the built matrices per slot.

GROUPS: GTYPE "cyclic" (Z_q), "abelian" (Z_A0 x Z_{q/A0}) or "custom".
A custom group is a code string defining valid_q(q) -> bool and
custom_ops(q) -> dict(elements, ident, mul, inv). Any finite group you
can write down qualifies; the scaffold validates identity, inverses and
associativity on samples before building.
"""
import json
import os
import random

N_MIN = int(os.environ.get("CM_NMIN", "100"))
N_MAX = int(os.environ.get("CM_NMAX", "600"))
W_MAX = int(os.environ.get("CM_WMAX", "12"))
CELL_MAX = 5
N_SLOTS = int(os.environ.get("CM_NSLOTS", "20"))
BASE_SEED = 20260903


# EVOLVE-BLOCK-START
CONCEPT = ("On cyclic Z19 of order 19 with A=(2,5), B=(2,5), twenty indexed gap-pool designs concentrated on parity-rank-one supports should produce five codes scoring above 20.443.")
GROUP = ("Keep the measured Z19 order-19 bed for a filling-only comparison: its n=551, k=179, d30s=8 slots each score 20.791.")
SHAPES = ("Keep equal (2,5)x(2,5) sides and equal-length X/Z component codes. Guaranteed k=19*(5-2)^2=171 gives rate 171/551=0.3103 in cell 0.300. The parent's lightest logicals were in sector-1 Acol/Bcol blocks, notably Acol1-2 for X and Bcol1-2 for Z.")
FILLING = ("Sixteen indexed motifs pair parity-rank-one support profiles; four are mixed-profile controls. Different A/B binomial-gap pools and more independent label trials are screened for short verified component relations and exact k. This tests whether five k=179 slots retain d30s=8 instead of the parent's frequent d2s=6 at that k.")
FAMILY_NAME = "r030_m1r19s1_25x25_rankone_gap_pools_v4"

GTYPE = "custom"
CUSTOM_GROUP = """
def valid_q(q):
    return q == 19

def custom_ops(q):
    # Same metacyclic presentation, with the order required by the new shape.
    m, r, s = 1, 19, 1
    els = [(a, b) for b in range(r) for a in range(m)]

    def mul(g, h):
        return ((g[0] + pow(s, g[1], m) * h[0]) % m, (g[1] + h[1]) % r)

    def inv(g):
        bi = (-g[1]) % r
        return ((-pow(s, bi, m) * g[0]) % m, bi)

    return dict(elements=els, ident=(0, 0), mul=mul, inv=inv)
"""
A0 = None                      # only used by GTYPE = "abelian"

A_SHAPE = (2, 5)               # (ra, ca)
B_SHAPE = (2, 5)               # (rb, cb)


def _support_profile(rng, kind):
    """Three distinct count profiles; permute columns before labelling."""
    profiles = (
        # Parity rank one: four (2,1) columns and one (0,3).
        [[2, 2, 2, 2, 0], [1, 1, 1, 1, 3]],
        # Parity rank two: rows 8/8, column degrees 3,3,4,3,3.
        [[2, 2, 2, 1, 1], [1, 1, 2, 2, 2]],
        # Parity rank two: rows 8/9, column degrees 3,3,3,4,4.
        [[2, 2, 2, 1, 1], [1, 1, 1, 3, 3]],
    )
    order = rng.sample(range(5), 5)
    rows = [[row[j] for j in order] for row in profiles[kind]]
    if rng.randrange(2):
        rows.reverse()
    return rows


def _label_grid(rng, support, motif, side):
    """Independently label cells using an indexed binomial-gap pool."""
    from itertools import combinations

    pools = tuple(combinations(range(1, 10), 6))
    preferred = pools[(17 * motif + 31 * side) % len(pools)]
    grid = []
    for row in support:
        used_gaps = set()
        labelled = []
        for count in row:
            if count == 0:
                labelled.append([])
                continue
            if count == 2:
                available = [gap for gap in preferred
                             if gap not in used_gaps]
                gap = rng.choice(available or preferred)
                start = rng.randrange(19)
                chosen = [start, (start + gap) % 19]
                used_gaps.add(gap)
            else:
                chosen = None
                for _ in range(100):
                    shifts = rng.sample(range(19), count)
                    gaps = {
                        min((x - y) % 19, (y - x) % 19)
                        for p, x in enumerate(shifts) for y in shifts[:p]
                    }
                    if len(gaps) == count * (count - 1) // 2 \
                            and not (gaps & used_gaps):
                        chosen = shifts
                        used_gaps.update(gaps)
                        break
                if chosen is None:
                    chosen = rng.sample(range(19), count)
            labelled.append([(0, shift) for shift in chosen])
        grid.append(labelled)
    return grid


def _component_columns(hx, hz, q):
    """Extract both two-row component checks from the verified LP lifts."""
    mask = (1 << q) - 1
    a_cols = [0] * (5 * q)
    b_cols = [0] * (5 * q)
    for i in range(2):
        for r in range(q):
            # HX row (Arow i, Bcol 0), restricted to its A blocks.
            a_row = hx[i * 5 * q + r]
            # HZ row (Acol 0, Brow i), restricted to its B blocks.
            b_row = hz[i * q + r]
            mark = 1 << (i * q + r)
            for j in range(5):
                bits = (a_row >> (j * 5 * q)) & mask
                while bits:
                    bit = bits & -bits
                    a_cols[j * q + bit.bit_length() - 1] |= mark
                    bits ^= bit
                bits = (b_row >> (j * q)) & mask
                while bits:
                    bit = bits & -bits
                    b_cols[j * q + bit.bit_length() - 1] |= mark
                    bits ^= bit
    return a_cols, b_cols


def _component_reading(columns, rng):
    """Detect component kernel words through six; sample weight seven."""
    size = len(columns)
    singles = set(columns)
    if 0 in singles:
        return 1
    if len(singles) != size:
        return 2

    pairs = set()
    for i in range(size):
        ci = columns[i]
        for j in range(i + 1, size):
            syndrome = ci ^ columns[j]
            if syndrome in singles:
                return 3
            if syndrome in pairs:
                return 4
            pairs.add(syndrome)

    triples = set()
    for i in range(size - 2):
        ci = columns[i]
        for j in range(i + 1, size - 1):
            cij = ci ^ columns[j]
            for k in range(j + 1, size):
                syndrome = cij ^ columns[k]
                if syndrome == 0:
                    return 3
                if syndrome in pairs:
                    return 5
                if syndrome in triples:
                    return 6
                triples.add(syndrome)

    for _ in range(30000):
        i, j, k, ell = rng.sample(range(size), 4)
        syndrome = (columns[i] ^ columns[j] ^
                    columns[k] ^ columns[ell])
        if syndrome in triples:
            return 7
    return 8                    # a screened reading, not a distance proof


def _minor_grade(hx, hz, q):
    """Grade three-column relations in both verified cyclic components."""
    from itertools import combinations

    mask = (1 << q) - 1

    def rotate(x, shift):
        return ((x << shift) | (x >> (q - shift))) & mask

    def product(x, y):
        result = 0
        while y:
            bit = y & -y
            result ^= rotate(x, bit.bit_length() - 1)
            y ^= bit
        return result

    def one_side(rows, stride):
        blocks = [[(rows[i] >> (j * stride * q)) & mask
                   for j in range(5)] for i in range(2)]
        words = []
        for columns in combinations(range(5), 3):
            parts = [0] * 5
            for position, j in enumerate(columns):
                u, v = [columns[t] for t in range(3) if t != position]
                parts[j] = (product(blocks[0][u], blocks[1][v]) ^
                            product(blocks[1][u], blocks[0][v]))
            word = sum(part << (q * j) for j, part in enumerate(parts))
            if not word:
                return (0, -10000, -10000)
            words.append(word)

        shifted = []
        for word in words:
            shifted.append([
                sum(rotate((word >> (q * j)) & mask, s) << (q * j)
                    for j in range(5))
                for s in range(q)
            ])

        minimum = min(word.bit_count() for word in words)
        seven = eight = 0
        for i, word in enumerate(words):
            weight = word.bit_count()
            seven += weight <= 7
            eight += weight == 8
            for j in range(i, len(words)):
                for other in shifted[j]:
                    combined = word ^ other
                    if not combined:
                        continue
                    weight = combined.bit_count()
                    minimum = min(minimum, weight)
                    seven += weight <= 7
                    eight += weight == 8
        return minimum, -seven, -eight

    a = one_side((hx[0], hx[5 * q]), 5)
    b = one_side((hz[0], hz[q]), 1)
    return (min(a[0], b[0]), a[0] + b[0],
            a[1] + b[1], a[2] + b[2])


def NEW_CTX(rng, G, idx):
    """Screen one indexed gap design and verify both components and k."""
    motif = idx % 20
    if motif < 16:
        a_kind, b_kind = 0, 0
    else:
        a_kind, b_kind = ((0, 1), (1, 0),
                          (0, 2), (2, 0))[motif - 16]
    sa = _support_profile(rng, a_kind)
    sb = _support_profile(rng, b_kind)
    assert _grid_weights(sa, sb) <= 12
    assert _no_dead_lines(sa, sb)

    trials = []
    for _ in range(48):
        a = _label_grid(rng, sa, motif, 0)
        b = _label_grid(rng, sb, motif, 1)
        hx, hz, n = lifted_matrices(G, a, b)
        minor = _minor_grade(hx, hz, G.q)
        trials.append((minor, rng.random(), a, b, hx, hz, n))
    trials.sort(key=lambda item: (item[0], item[1]), reverse=True)

    best = best_key = None
    for minor, tie, a, b, hx, hz, n in trials[:12]:
        acols, bcols = _component_columns(hx, hz, G.q)
        da = _component_reading(acols, rng)
        db = _component_reading(bcols, rng)
        k = n - gf2_rank(hx) - gf2_rank(hz)
        # k=179 remains below this cell's 0.325 upper rate edge.
        key = (int(171 <= k <= 179), min(da, db),
               int(k == 179), k, minor, da + db, tie)
        if best_key is None or key > best_key:
            best_key = key
            best = {"A": a, "B": b}
    return best


def CELL(rng, G, side, i, j, ctx):
    return list(ctx[side][i][j])

# REFUTED LEDGER (append-only; one line per hypothesis killed by
# measured evidence - never delete or rewrite existing lines):
# (empty)
# Uniform rolled supports do not reliably avoid short witnesses: parent
# slots 1 and 2 had d2s=2 despite satisfying the row-weight cap.
# Screening only kernel words through weight four does not eliminate the
# distance-7/8 sector-1 witnesses: all five measured parent slots retained them.
# REFUTED: Zero/trinomial exchanges with short-component screening suffice
# for d30s>8: all five current-parent measured slots retained d30s=8.
# REFUTED: The parent's generic minor screen reliably preserves distance
# eight on rank-one-parity fillings: eight of its ten k=179 slots had d2s=6.
# EVOLVE-BLOCK-END


class _G(object):
    def __init__(self, ops, q):
        self.q = q
        self.elements = list(ops["elements"])
        self.e = ops["ident"]
        self.mul = ops["mul"]
        self.inv = ops["inv"]
        self._pos = {self._key(g): i for i, g in enumerate(self.elements)}

    @staticmethod
    def _key(g):
        return tuple(g) if isinstance(g, (list, tuple)) else g

    def idx(self, g):
        return self._pos[self._key(g)]

    def pick(self, rng):
        return self.elements[rng.randrange(self.q)]


# ---- verified construction helpers (2026-09-09) --------------------------
# lifted_matrices(G, cells_A, cells_B) builds EXACTLY the HX / HZ the
# evaluator builds (same lift conventions, same column order), as Python-int
# bit rows; gf2_rank(rows) is the rank behind k; lifted_k(G, A, B) is the k the table will report;
# css_ok(hx, hz) checks HX HZ^T = 0. Use these for any in-program screening -
# a hand-written lift that gets the left/right actions wrong screens a
# different (usually non-CSS) matrix pair than the one that is measured.
def lifted_matrices(G, cells_A, cells_B):
    """cells_A[i][j], cells_B[a][b]: lists of group elements (as CELL returns
    them) or element indices. Returns (hx_rows, hz_rows, n): lists of Python
    ints, bit c set = a 1 in column c; n = number of columns (qubits)."""
    q = G.q
    ra, ca = len(cells_A), len(cells_A[0])
    rb, cb = len(cells_B), len(cells_B[0])
    els = G.elements

    def gi(g):
        try:
            return G.idx(g)
        except (KeyError, TypeError):
            if isinstance(g, int) and 0 <= g < q:
                return g
            raise
    mul_idx = [[G.idx(G.mul(els[x], els[y])) for y in range(q)] for x in range(q)]
    inv_idx = [G.idx(G.inv(els[x])) for x in range(q)]
    A = [[sorted(set(gi(g) for g in cell)) for cell in row] for row in cells_A]
    B = [[sorted(set(gi(g) for g in cell)) for cell in row] for row in cells_B]
    n1 = ca * cb * q
    n = n1 + ra * rb * q
    hx = []
    for i in range(ra):
        for beta in range(cb):
            for r in range(q):
                row = 0
                for j in range(ca):                       # sector 1: A_{ij} left-lifted
                    base = (j * cb + beta) * q
                    for g in A[i][j]:
                        row ^= 1 << (base + mul_idx[inv_idx[g]][r])   # h with g*h = r
                for alpha in range(rb):                   # sector 2: B_{alpha beta}^T right-lifted
                    base = n1 + (i * rb + alpha) * q
                    for g in B[alpha][beta]:
                        row ^= 1 << (base + mul_idx[r][inv_idx[g]])   # c = r*g^-1
                hx.append(row)
    hz = []
    for j in range(ca):
        for alpha in range(rb):
            for r in range(q):
                row = 0
                for beta in range(cb):                    # sector 1: B_{alpha beta} right-lifted
                    base = (j * cb + beta) * q
                    for g in B[alpha][beta]:
                        row ^= 1 << (base + mul_idx[r][g])           # h with h*g^-1 = r
                for i in range(ra):                       # sector 2: A_{ij}^T left-lifted
                    base = n1 + (i * rb + alpha) * q
                    for g in A[i][j]:
                        row ^= 1 << (base + mul_idx[g][r])           # c = g*r
                hz.append(row)
    return hx, hz, n


def _popcount(x):
    return bin(x).count("1")



def gf2_rank(rows):
    """Rank over GF(2) of a list of bit rows."""
    pivots = {}
    for row in rows:
        while row:
            p = row.bit_length() - 1
            if p in pivots:
                row ^= pivots[p]
            else:
                pivots[p] = row
                break
    return len(pivots)


def lifted_k(G, cells_A, cells_B):
    """k = n - rank(HX) - rank(HZ) of the code the evaluator will build."""
    hx, hz, n = lifted_matrices(G, cells_A, cells_B)
    return n - gf2_rank(hx) - gf2_rank(hz)


def css_ok(hx, hz):
    """True iff every X check commutes with every Z check (HX HZ^T = 0)."""
    return all(_popcount(x & z) % 2 == 0 for x in hx for z in hz)


def _grid_weights(sa, sb):
    ra, ca = A_SHAPE
    rb, cb = B_SHAPE
    wx = max(sum(sa[i]) + sum(sb[r_][j] for r_ in range(rb))
             for i in range(ra) for j in range(cb))
    wz = max(sum(sb[r_]) + sum(sa[i][c] for i in range(ra))
             for r_ in range(rb) for c in range(ca))
    return max(wx, wz)


def _no_dead_lines(sa, sb):
    return all(
        all(sum(row) > 0 for row in g)
        and all(sum(r_[c] for r_ in g) > 0 for c in range(len(g[0])))
        for g in (sa, sb))


def roll_supports(rng):
    """Random per-cell term counts (1..CELL_MAX), repaired down to the
    weight cap with 1 as the floor - the roller never emits zero blocks
    (a slot's CELL may still return [] deliberately). A scaffold
    utility available to NEW_CTX; always returns legal grids."""
    ra, ca = A_SHAPE
    rb, cb = B_SHAPE
    sa = [[rng.randint(1, CELL_MAX) for _ in range(ca)] for _ in range(ra)]
    sb = [[rng.randint(1, CELL_MAX) for _ in range(cb)] for _ in range(rb)]
    for _ in range(4000):
        if _grid_weights(sa, sb) <= W_MAX:
            return sa, sb
        g = sa if rng.random() < 0.5 else sb
        i = rng.randrange(len(g))
        c = rng.randrange(len(g[0]))
        if g[i][c] >= 2:            # 1 is the floor: the roller never
            g[i][c] -= 1            # emits zero blocks (evolution may)
    raise ValueError("roll_supports could not fit the weight cap "
                     "(all-ones row exceeds it: shape too wide)")


def _registry_ops(gtype, q, a0):
    if gtype == "cyclic":
        return dict(elements=list(range(q)), ident=0,
                    mul=lambda g, h: (g + h) % q,
                    inv=lambda g: (-g) % q)
    if gtype == "abelian":
        a = int(a0)
        if a < 1 or q % a:
            raise ValueError("A0 must divide q")
        b = q // a
        return dict(elements=[(x, y) for x in range(a) for y in range(b)],
                    ident=(0, 0),
                    mul=lambda g, h: ((g[0] + h[0]) % a, (g[1] + h[1]) % b),
                    inv=lambda g: ((-g[0]) % a, (-g[1]) % b))
    raise ValueError("GTYPE %r needs CUSTOM_GROUP" % gtype)


def _make_ops(q):
    if GTYPE == "custom":
        ns = {}
        exec(CUSTOM_GROUP, ns)
        if not ns["valid_q"](q):
            raise ValueError("valid_q rejects q=%d" % q)
        return ns["custom_ops"](q)
    return _registry_ops(GTYPE, q, A0)


def _valid_q(q):
    if GTYPE == "custom":
        ns = {}
        exec(CUSTOM_GROUP, ns)
        return bool(ns["valid_q"](q))
    if GTYPE == "abelian":
        return A0 is not None and int(A0) >= 1 and q % int(A0) == 0
    return q >= 2


def _validate_group(G):
    rng = random.Random(7)
    sample = [G.e] + [G.pick(rng) for _ in range(min(12, G.q))]
    for g in sample:
        assert G._key(G.mul(G.e, g)) == G._key(g), "identity fails"
        assert G._key(G.mul(g, G.inv(g))) == G._key(G.e), "inverse fails"
    for g in sample[:6]:
        for h in sample[:6]:
            for k in sample[:6]:
                assert G._key(G.mul(G.mul(g, h), k)) \
                    == G._key(G.mul(g, G.mul(h, k))), "associativity fails"
    assert len(G._pos) == len(G.elements) == G.q, "element list broken"


def _choose_q():
    ra, ca = A_SHAPE
    rb, cb = B_SHAPE
    if min(ra, ca, rb, cb) < 1:
        raise ValueError("protograph sides need >= 1 row and >= 1 column")
    f = ca * cb + ra * rb
    best = None
    for q in range(max(2, N_MIN // f), N_MAX // f + 1):
        if f * q < N_MIN or f * q >= N_MAX:
            continue
        if _valid_q(q):
            best = q
    if best is None:
        raise ValueError("no valid q puts n in [%d, %d)" % (N_MIN, N_MAX))
    return best


def _fill_slot(rng, G, idx):
    ctx = NEW_CTX(rng, G, idx)
    grids = {}
    rgrids = {}
    for side, (rows, cols) in (("A", A_SHAPE), ("B", B_SHAPE)):
        grid = []
        rgrid = []
        for i in range(rows):
            row = []
            rrow = []
            for j in range(cols):
                cell = CELL(rng, G, side, i, j, ctx)
                if not isinstance(cell, (list, tuple)):
                    raise ValueError("%s[%d][%d]: not a list" % (side, i, j))
                if len(cell) > CELL_MAX:
                    raise ValueError("%s[%d][%d]: >%d terms"
                                     % (side, i, j, CELL_MAX))
                keys = {G._key(g) for g in cell}
                if len(keys) != len(cell):
                    raise ValueError("%s[%d][%d]: duplicate element"
                                     % (side, i, j))
                pairs = sorted((G.idx(g), G._key(g)) for g in cell)
                row.append([i for i, _ in pairs])
                rrow.append([list(e) if isinstance(e, tuple) else e
                             for _, e in pairs])
            grid.append(row)
            rgrid.append(rrow)
        grids[side] = grid
        rgrids[side] = rgrid
    sa = [[len(c) for c in row] for row in grids["A"]]
    sb = [[len(c) for c in row] for row in grids["B"]]
    w = _grid_weights(sa, sb)
    if w > W_MAX:
        raise ValueError("row weight %d exceeds cap %d" % (w, W_MAX))
    if not _no_dead_lines(sa, sb):
        raise ValueError("all-zero protograph row or column")
    return grids["A"], grids["B"], rgrids["A"], rgrids["B"]


def get_candidates():
    """-> request dict for the worker: shared group data + 20 slots."""
    q = _choose_q()
    ops = _make_ops(q)
    G = _G(ops, q)
    _validate_group(G)
    mul_table = [[G.idx(G.mul(G.elements[a], G.elements[b]))
                  for b in range(q)] for a in range(q)]
    ra, ca = A_SHAPE
    rb, cb = B_SHAPE
    slots = []
    for s in range(N_SLOTS):
        rng = random.Random(BASE_SEED * 10007 + s)
        try:
            A, B, Ar, Br = _fill_slot(rng, G, s)
            slots.append({"idx": s, "A": A, "B": B, "Ar": Ar, "Br": Br})
        except Exception as e:
            slots.append({"idx": s, "error": str(e)[:120]})
    return {"family": FAMILY_NAME, "concept": CONCEPT,
            "gtype": GTYPE, "q": q, "mul": mul_table,
            "a_shape": [ra, ca], "b_shape": [rb, cb],
            "n": (ca * cb + ra * rb) * q,
            "slots": slots}


if __name__ == "__main__":
    r = get_candidates()
    ok = sum(1 for s in r["slots"] if "error" not in s)
    print(json.dumps({"family": r["family"], "q": r["q"], "n": r["n"],
                      "slots_ok": ok}))
```
